     The Group achieved a consolidated revenue of RMB5,620            本 集 團 於 截 至2022年12月31日 止 的 年 度 共
     million for the year ended December 31, 2022 (2021:              實 現 綜 合 營 業 額 人 民 幣56.20億 元（2021年
     RMB4,447 million); the increase in revenue was mainly            度：人民幣44.47億元）；營業額的相關增長
     attributable to the improved operating efficiency as well as     一方面是因為經營效益改善，另一方面亦因
     the effects in relation to the consolidation of certain          完成多家醫院的併表安排而產生。
     hospitals.

     During the Reporting Period, the Group recorded a net profit     而報告期內本集團錄得淨利潤為人民幣1.59
     of RMB159 million (2021: RMB426 million) and earnings per        億元（2021年度：人民幣4.26億元）及每股盈
     share of RMB0.11 (2021: RMB0.33 per share). Net profit           利 人 民 幣0.11元（2021年 度：每 股 人 民 幣
     decreased 62.6% year-on-year, which was mainly                   0.33元）。淨利潤同比下降62.6%，主要由於
     attributable to the operating losses and impairments incurred    本集團於2021年收購淮陰醫院所產生的經營
     in relation to the equity interests of the Huaiyin Hospital,     虧損與減值，以及由於報告期內人民幣較大
     which was acquired by the Group in 2021, as well as              幅度貶值形成的匯兌損失所致。
     exchange losses due to the relatively significant depreciation
     of the RMB during the Reporting Period.

     In 2022, the material impairments to the accounting items        2022年內本集團錄得較大的減值會計項目包
     of the Group include impairment of approximately RMB108          括淮陰醫院項目商譽減值人民幣1.08億元，
     million in respect of the goodwill of the Huaiyin Hospital       淮陰醫院應收政府購買服務費計提壞賬準備
     project. The provision for Huaiyin Hospital’s government         人民幣0.45億元，相關減值為非現金及非經
     purchase service fee receivables is approximately RMB45          常性質。此外，對本集團持有的港幣22億元
     million, which were non-cash and non-recurring in nature. In     外幣貸款形成人民幣1.59億元的匯兌損失，
     addition, because of the impacts of the relatively significant   彼亦為非現金性質。
     depreciation of RMB during the Reporting Period on the

                                                                  管理層討論與分析



Consolidating the Wugang Hospital Group                           併表武鋼醫院集團
Since the corresponding documents in relation to the daily        按本公司與華潤武鋼合營公司簽立的與華潤
operation arrangement of CR Wugang JV have been                   武鋼合營公司日常營運安排有關的相應文
executed between it and the Company, the Group has                件，本集團具備獨立指導華潤武鋼合營公司

     Impairments                                                    減值安排
     For the fiscal year ended December 31, 2022, Huaiyin           截 至2022年12月31日 止 之 財 政 年 度，淮 陰
     Hospital has impaired receivables due from the local           醫院向當地政府提供醫療服務的應收款項計
     government of approximately RMB45 million in respect of        提壞賬準備約人民幣4,500萬元。淮陰醫院自
     the medical services provided to the local government.         2015年起向當地政府提供有關服務，該等服
     Huaiyin Hospital has provided the relevant services to the     務和相應的服務費用金額必須經當地政府聘
     local government since 2015, where such services and the       請的獨立審計師完成審計及確認後方能予以
     corresponding service fee amounts have to be audited and       支付。當地政府已支付部分已經核定金額之
     confirmed by the local government’s independent auditor        服務費。收購淮陰醫院項目交割時，本公司
     before such amounts could be settled. The local                認為收購標的賬面上對當地政府的應收款為
     government has paid portions of the reviewed and               可以收回的款項。惟近兩年該等政府的審計
     confirmed service fees. At the completion of the acquisition   工作有所延誤且本公司需要與當地政府持續
     of Huaiyin Hospital, the Company considered that the           溝通以獲取審計結果的反饋，導致應收款項
     concerned receivables from the local government on the         結算延遲。本公司一直關注該等應收款的收
     books of the acquisition target were recoverable. However,     回情況、並與當地政府相關部門積極溝通。
     in the past two years, the government audit works have         然而，由於2022年政府審計工作再有延誤，
     been delayed and the Company needs to communicate with         本公司因此於2022年財政年度末對該等應收
     the local government continuously to obtain feedback on the    款再進行了撥備。在該收購項目交割前，本
     audit results, resulting in delays in settlement of the        公司已按其適用的內部程序就上述收購淮陰
     receivables. The Company has been reviewing the status of      醫院進行了法律和財務方面的盡職調查。本
     recovery of such receivables and actively communicating        公司並不知悉亦沒有預計到任何前述導致該
     with the relevant local government departments in respect      等應收賬款於2022年財政年度減值的因素。

and Other Risks

the Hong Kong dollar and U.S. dollar against RMB.

against its exposure to currency risk. The management             管理層透過密切監控外匯匯率變動來管理貨

movement of the foreign currency rates and considers              重大外匯風險進行對沖。
hedging against significant foreign exchange exposure
should such need arise.

criminal, in which judgment is given in his/her favor, or in
which he is acquitted.

practice under the Listing Rules and other relevant
regulatory requirements.

professional development to develop and refresh their            發展及更新彼等之知識及技能。董事獲持續
knowledge and skills. Directors are kept updated on the          提供法定及監管發展，以及業務及市況變遷

business and the market so as to facilitate the discharge of     年12月31日止年度，全體董事參與有關上市
their responsibilities. All Directors have participated in the   公司董事之角色、職能及職責之課程，或透

Opinion                                                        意見
We have audited the consolidated financial statements of       我們已審計列載於第117至287頁的華潤醫療
China Resources Medical Holdings Company Limited (the          控股有限公司（「貴公司」）及其附屬公司（「貴
“Company”) and its subsidiaries (the “Group”) set out on       集團」）的綜合財務報表，此綜合財務報表包
pages 117 to 287, which comprise the consolidated              括 於2022年12月31日 的 綜 合 財 務 狀 況 報 表
statement of financial position as at 31 December 2022,        與截至該日止年度的綜合損益報表、綜合全
and the consolidated statement of profit or loss, the          面收益報表、綜合權益變動報表和綜合現金
consolidated statement of comprehensive income, the            流量報表，以及綜合財務報表附註，包括主
consolidated statement of changes in equity and the            要會計政策概要。
consolidated statement of cash flows for the year then
ended, and notes to the consolidated financial statements,
including a summary of significant accounting policies.

In our opinion, the consolidated financial statements give a 我們認為，該等綜合財務報表已根據國際會
true and fair view of the consolidated financial position of 計準則理事會頒布的《國際財務報告準則》真
the Group as at 31 December 2022, and of its consolidated 實 而 中 肯 地 反 映 了 貴 集 團 於2022年12月
financial performance and its consolidated cash flows for the 31日的綜合財務狀況及截至該日止年度的綜
year then ended in accordance with International Financial 合財務表現及綜合現金流量，並已遵照香港
Reporting Standards (“IFRSs”) issued by the International 《公司條例》的披露規定妥為擬備。
Accounting Standards Board (“IASB”) and have been
properly prepared in compliance with the disclosure
requirements of the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.

Basis for opinion                                              意見的基礎
We conducted our audit in accordance with Hong Kong            我們已根據香港會計師公會頒布的《香港審
Standards on Auditing (“HKSAs”) issued by the Hong Kong        計準則》進行審計。我們在該等準則下承擔
Institute of Certified Public Accountants (“HKICPA”). Our      的責任已在本報告「核數師就審計綜合財務
responsibilities under those standards are further described   報表承擔的責任 」部分中作進一步闡述。根
in the Auditor’s responsibilities for the audit of the         據香港會計師公會頒布的《專業會計師道德
consolidated financial statements section of our report. We    守則》
                                                                 （以下簡稱「守則」），我們獨立於 貴集
are independent of the Group in accordance with the            團，並已履行守則中的其他專業道德責任。
HKICPA’s Code of Ethics for Professional Accountants (the      我們相信，我們所獲得的審計憑證能充足及
“Code”), and we have fulfilled our other ethical               適當地為我們的審計意見提供基礎。
responsibilities in accordance with the Code. We believe
that the audit evidence we have obtained is sufficient and
appropriate to provide a basis for our opinion.

      Auditor’s responsibilities for the audit of                      核數師就審計綜合財務報表承擔
      the consolidated financial statements                            的責任
      Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about          我們的目標，是對綜合財務報表整體是否不
      whether the consolidated financial statements as a whole         存在由於欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述
      are free from material misstatement, whether due to fraud        取得合理保證，並出具包括我們意見的核數
      or error, and to issue an auditor’s report that includes our     師報告。我們僅對全體股東作出報告，除此
      opinion. Our report is made solely to you, as a body, and for    以外，本報告並無其他用途。我們不會就核
      no other purpose. We do not assume responsibility towards        數師報告的內容向任何其他人士負上或承擔
      or accept liability to any other person for the contents of      任何責任。
      this report.

      Reasonable assurance is a high level of assurance, but is not 合理保證是高水平的保證，但不能保證按照
      a guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance with 《香港審計準則》進行的審計，在某一重大錯
      HKSAs will always detect a material misstatement when it 誤陳述存在時總能發現。錯誤陳述可以由欺
      exists. Misstatements can arise from fraud or error and are 詐或錯誤引起，如果合理預期它們單獨或匯
      considered material if, individually or in the aggregate, they 總起來可能影響綜合財務報表使用者依賴綜
      could reasonably be expected to influence the economic 合財務報表所作出的經濟決定，則有關的錯
      decisions of users taken on the basis of these consolidated 誤陳述可被視作重大。
      financial statements.

      professional judgement and maintain professional scepticism      我 們 運 用 了 專 業 判 斷，保 持 了 專 業 懷 疑 態
      throughout the audit. We also:                                   度。我們亦：

           responsive to those risks, and obtain audit evidence that        及獲取充足和適當的審計憑證，作為我
           is sufficient and appropriate to provide a basis for our         們 意 見 的 基 礎。由 於 欺 詐 可 能 涉 及 串
           opinion. The risk of not detecting a material                    謀、偽造、蓄意遺漏、虛假陳述，或凌
           misstatement resulting from fraud is higher than for             駕於內部控制之上，因此未能發現因欺
           one resulting from error, as fraud may involve collusion,        詐而導致的重大錯誤陳述的風險高於未

           the override of internal control.                                風險。

                 the contract as well as contract management and                  合同並無直接關係，除非合同訂
                 supervision costs). General and administrative                   明可向對手方收取，否則將排除
                 costs do not relate directly to a contract and are               在外。本集團已按未來基準對截
                 excluded unless they are explicitly chargeable to                至2022年1月1日其尚未履行全部
                 the counterparty under the contract. The Group                   義務的合同應用該等修訂，且並
                 has applied the amendments prospectively to                      無識別到任何繁苛合同。因此，
                 contracts for which it has not yet fulfilled all its             該等修訂並無對本集團的財務狀
                 obligations at 1 January 2022 and no onerous                     況或表現產生任何影響。
                 contracts were identified. Therefore, the
                 amendments did not have any impact on the
                 financial position or performance of the Group.

2.4 SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT                                    2.4 主要會計政策概要
    ACCOUNTING POLICIES
  Investments in associates and joint                           於聯營公司及合營公司之投資
  ventures
  An associate is an entity in which the Group has a            聯營公司為本集團於其一般不少於20%
  long term interest of generally not less than 20% of          股本投票權中擁有長期權益的實體，且
  the equity voting rights and over which it is in a            可對其發揮重大影響力。重大影響力指
  position to exercise significant influence. Significant       的是參與投資對象的財務和經營決策的
  influence is the power to participate in the financial        權力，但不是控制或共同控制這些決策
  and operating policy decisions of the investee, but is        的權力。
  not control or joint control over those policies.

  A joint venture is a type of joint arrangement whereby        合營公司指一類共同安排，對安排擁有
  the parties that have joint control of the arrangement        共同控制權之訂約方據此有權享有合營
  have rights to the net assets of the joint venture. Joint     公司之資產淨值。共同控制指對某項安
  control is the contractually agreed sharing of control of     排之訂約協定共同控制，共同控制僅於
  an arrangement, which exists only when decisions              相關活動之決定需共享控制權之各方一
  about the relevant activities require the unanimous           致同意之時存在。
  consent of the parties sharing control.

  The Group’s investments in associates and a joint             本集團於聯營公司及合營公司的投資乃
  venture are stated in the consolidated statement of           按本集團根據權益會計法應佔資產淨值

  under the equity method of accounting, less any               賬。倘會計政策存在任何不一致，將會
  impairment losses. Adjustments are made to bring into         作出相應調整。本集團應佔聯營公司及
  line any dissimilar accounting policies that may exist.       合營公司收購後業績及其他全面收入分
  The Group’s share of the post-acquisition results and         別計入綜合損益報表及綜合其他全面收
  other comprehensive income of associates and a joint          入。此外，倘於聯營公司或合營公司的

  profit or loss and consolidated other comprehensive           權益變動報表確認其應佔任何變動（倘
  income, respectively. In addition, when there has been        適用）。本集團與其聯營公司或合營公
  a change recognised directly in the equity of the             司間交易的未變現收益及虧損將以本集
  associate or joint venture, the Group recognises its          團於聯營公司或合營公司的投資為限對

  consolidated statement of changes in equity.                  值則除外。收購聯營公司或合營公司所
  Unrealised gains and losses resulting from transactions       產生的商譽已計入作本集團於聯營公司

2.4 SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT                                      2.4 主要會計政策概要（續）
    ACCOUNTING POLICIES (continued)
   Fair value measurement                                         公允價值計量
   The Group measures its financial assets at FVTPL at            本集團於各報告期末按公允價值計量以
   fair value at the end of each reporting period. Fair           公允價值計量且其變動計入損益的金融
   value is the price that would be received to sell an           資產。公允價值乃在市場參與者於計量
   asset or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly            日期進行的有序交易中出售資產所收取
   transaction between market participants at the                 或轉移負債所支付的價格。公允價值計
   measurement date. The fair value measurement is                量乃基於假設出售資產或轉移負債的交
   based on the presumption that the transaction to sell          易於資產或負債的主要市場進行或於未
   the asset or transfer the liability takes place either in      有主要市場的情況下，則於資產或負債
   the principal market for the asset or liability, or in the     的最有利市場進行。主要或最有利市場
   absence of a principal market, in the most                     須位於本集團能到達的地方。資產或負
   advantageous market for the asset or liability. The            債的公允價值乃基於市場參與者為資產
   principal or the most advantageous market must be              或負債定價所用的假設計量（假設市場
   accessible by the Group. The fair value of an asset or         參與者依照彼等的最佳經濟利益行事）
                                                                                  。
   a liability is measured using the assumptions that
   market participants would use when pricing the asset
   or liability, assuming that market participants act in
   their economic best interest.

   The Group uses valuation techniques that are                   本集團使用適用於不同情況的估值方
   appropriate in the circumstances and for which                 法，而其有足夠資料計量公允價值，以
   sufficient data are available to measure fair value,           盡量使用相關可觀察輸入數據及盡量減
   maximising the use of relevant observable inputs and           少使用不可觀察輸入數據。
   minimising the use of unobservable inputs.

           significant financing component, the Group initially              計量且其變動計入損益）交易成本計量

           or loss, transaction costs. Trade receivables that do not         國際財務報告準則第15號釐定的交易

           the Group has applied the practical expedient are                 策一致。
           measured at the transaction price determined under
           IFRS 15 in accordance with the policies set out for
           “Revenue recognition” below.

           In order for a financial asset to be classified and               金融資產需產生純粹為支付本金及未償
           measured at amortised cost, it needs to give rise to              還本金利息（「純粹為支付本金及利息」）
           cash flows that are solely payments of principal and              的現金流量，方可按攤銷成本進行分類
           interest (“SPPI”) on the principal amount outstanding.            及計量。現金流量不是純粹為支付本金
           Financial assets with cash flows that are not SPPI are            及利息的金融資產被分類為以公允價值
           classified and measured at fair value through profit or           計量且其變動計入損益並按此計量，與
           loss, irrespective of the business model.                         業務模式無關。

           The Group’s business model for managing financial                 本集團管理金融資產的業務模式指其如
           assets refers to how it manages its financial assets in           何管理其金融資產以產生現金流量。業
           order to generate cash flows. The business model                  務模式確定現金流量是否來自收集合約
           determines whether cash flows will result from                    現 金 流 量、出 售 金 融 資 產，或 兩 者 兼
           collecting contractual cash flows, selling the financial          有。以攤銷成本分類和計量的金融資產
           assets, or both. Financial assets classified and                  在一種業務模式中持有，其目的是持有
           measured at amortised cost are held within a business             金融資產以收集合同現金流量。不屬於
           model with the objective to hold financial assets in              上述業務模式的金融資產，乃分類為以
           order to collect contractual cash flows. Financial assets         公允價值計量且其變動計入損益並按此
           which are not held within the aforementioned business             計量。
           model are classified and measured at fair value
           through profit or loss.

          ACCOUNTING POLICIES (continued)
           Impairment of financial assets                                    金融資產減值
           The Group recognises an allowance for expected credit             本集團就並非以公允價值計量且其變動
           losses (“ECLs”) for all debt instruments not held at fair         計入損益的所有債務工具確認預期信貸
           value through profit or loss. ECLs are based on the               虧 損（「預 期 信 貸 虧 損」）的 撥 備。預 期
           difference between the contractual cash flows due in              信貸虧損乃基於根據合約到期的合約現
           accordance with the contract and all the cash flows               金流量與本集團預期收取的所有現金流
           that the Group expects to receive, discounted at an               量之間的差額而釐定，並以原實際利率
           approximation of the original effective interest rate. The        的近似值貼現。預期現金流量將包括出
           expected cash flows will include cash flows from the              售所持抵押品或組成合約條款的其他信
           sale of collateral held or other credit enhancements              貸提升措施的現金流量。
           that are integral to the contractual terms.

           General approach                                                  一般方法
           ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit                     預期信貸虧損分兩個階段確認。就自首
           exposures for which there has not been a significant              次確認起未有顯著增加的信貸風險而
           increase in credit risk since initial recognition, ECLs are       言，須 為 未 來12個 月 內 可 能 發 生 違 約
           provided for credit losses that result from default               事件而導致的信貸虧損（12個月預期信
           events that are possible within the next 12 months (a             貸虧損）計提預期信貸虧損。就自初始
           12-month ECL). For those credit exposures for which               確認起經已顯著增加的信貸風險而言，
           there has been a significant increase in credit risk              不論何時發生違約，於餘下風險年期內
           since initial recognition, a loss allowance is required for       的預期信貸虧損均須計提虧損撥備（全
           credit losses expected over the remaining life of the             期預期信貸虧損）。
           exposure, irrespective of the timing of the default (a
           lifetime ECL).

           At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether                於各報告日期，本集團評估金融工具的
           the credit risk on a financial instrument has increased           信貸風險自首次確認以來是否大幅增
           significantly since initial recognition. When making the          加。進行評估時，本集團會比較報告日
           assessment, the Group compares the risk of a default              期就金融工具產生的違約風險與於首次
           occurring on the financial instrument as at the                   確認日期就金融工具產生的違約風險，
           reporting date with the risk of a default occurring on            並會考慮合理及有理據且毋須花費過度
           the financial instrument as at the date of initial                成本或精力即可獲得的資料，包括歷史
           recognition and considers reasonable and supportable              及前瞻性資料。本集團認為，當合同付
           information that is available without undue cost or               款 逾 期 超 過30天 時，信 用 風 險 顯 著 增
           effort, including historical and forward-looking                  加。
           information. The Group considers that there has been
           a significant increase in credit risk when contractual
           payments are more than 30 days past due.

2.4 SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT                                        2.4 主要會計政策概要（續）
    ACCOUNTING POLICIES (continued)
   Derecognition of financial liabilities                           終止確認金融負債
   A financial liability is derecognised when the obligation        如果金融負債的責任已解除、取消或屆
   under the liability is discharged or cancelled, or               滿，則對該負債進行終止確認。
   expires.

   When an existing financial liability is replaced by              如果現有金融負債被同一貸款方以大部
   another from the same lender on substantially different          分條款不同的另一金融負債所取代，或
   terms, or the terms of an existing liability are                 者現有負債的條款經大幅修改，則此類
   substantially modified, such an exchange or                      替換或修改作為終止確認原負債和確認
   modification is treated as a derecognition of the                新負債處理，且各自賬面值的差異會在
   original liability and a recognition of a new liability, and     損益表中確認。
   the difference between the respective carrying
   amounts is recognised in the statement of profit or
   loss.

   Offsetting of financial instruments                              金融工具的抵銷
   Financial assets and financial liabilities are offset and        當目前擁有可強制執行的合法權利以抵
   the net amount is reported in the statement of                   銷已確認金額，且有意按淨額結算或同
   financial position if there is a currently enforceable           時變現資產及償還負債，方會抵銷金融
   legal right to offset the recognised amounts and there           資產及金融負債，並於財務狀況報表中
   is an intention to settle on a net basis, or to realise          呈列淨額。
   the assets and settle the liabilities simultaneously.

   Treasury shares                                                  庫存股份
   Own equity instruments which are reacquired and held             由本公司或本集團重新購入而持有之自
   by the Company or the Group (treasury shares) are                有權益工具（庫存股份）乃按成本直接
   recognised directly in equity at cost. No gain or loss is        在權益中確認。本集團就自有權益工具
   recognised in the statement of profit or loss on the             之購買、出售、發行或註銷，概無於損
   purchase, sale, issue or cancellation of the Group’s             益表確認任何收益或虧損。
   own equity instruments.

   Inventories                                                      存貨
   Inventories are stated at the lower of cost and net              存貨按成本與可變現淨值之較低者列
   realisable value. Cost is determined on the first-in,            賬。存貨成本採用先進先出法釐定。可
   first-out basis. Net realisable value is based on                變現淨值則按估計售價減於出售時將產
   estimated selling prices less any estimated costs to be          生的任何估計成本計算。
   incurred to disposal.

           Provisions                                                      撥備
           A provision is recognised when a present obligation             如因過往事件產生現有責任（法定或推
           (legal or constructive) has arisen as a result of a past        定），且資源可能須於日後流出以履行
           event and it is probable that a future outflow of               責任，而且該項責任的金額能可靠估計
           resources will be required to settle the obligation,            時，則確認撥備。
           provided that a reliable estimate can be made of the
           amount of the obligation.

           When the effect of discounting is material, the amount          如貼現影響屬重大，則就撥備確認的金
           recognised for a provision is the present value at the          額為履行責任預期所需的未來支出於報
           end of the reporting period of the future expenditures          告期間結算日的現值。已貼現現值因時
           expected to be required to settle the obligation. The           間流逝而產生的增幅於損益表確認。
           increase in the discounted present value amount
           arising from the passage of time is recognised in the
           statement of profit or loss.

           exactly discounts the estimated future cash receipts             間（如適用）將估計未來現金收款準確
           over the expected life of the financial instrument or a          貼現至金融資產賬面值淨額的利率。
           shorter period, when appropriate, to the net carrying
           amount of the financial asset.

           Dividend income is recognised when the shareholders’             股息收入於股東獲派股息權利確立、與
           right to receive payment has been established, it is             股息有關的經濟利益很可能會流入本集
           probable that the economic benefits associated with              團，並且股息的金額能夠可靠地計量時
           the dividend will flow to the Group and the amount of            確認。
           the dividend can be measured reliably.

3. SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING                                     3. 主要會計判斷及估計
   JUDGEMENTS AND ESTIMATES
   The preparation of the Group’s financial statements          本集團財務報表的編製要求管理層作出
   requires management to make judgements, estimates            判斷、估計和假設，該等判斷、估計和
   and assumptions that affect the reported amounts of          假設會影響收益、支出、資產和負債的
   revenues, expenses, assets and liabilities, and their        報告金額，及其伴隨而來的披露以及或
   accompanying disclosures, and the disclosure of              有負債的披露。這些假設和估計的不確
   contingent liabilities. Uncertainty about these              定性可能導致可能需要對未來受影響的
   assumptions and estimates could result in outcomes           資產或負債的賬面值進行重大調整。
   that could require a material adjustment to the carrying
   amounts of the assets or liabilities affected in the

           manage and operate the hospitals and receive                    院並收取績效管理費的權利。預計經營

           Leases — Estimating the incremental borrowing                     租賃 — 估計增量借貸利率
           rate
           The Group cannot readily determine the interest rate             本集團無法輕易釐定租賃的隱含利率，
           implicit in a lease, and therefore, it uses an                   因此使用增量借貸利率（「增量借貸 利
           incremental borrowing rate (“IBR”) to measure lease              率」）來計量租賃負債。增量借貸利率是
           liabilities. The IBR is the rate of interest that the Group      本集團在類似經濟環境下獲得與使用權
           would have to pay to borrow over a similar term, and             資產價值相近的資產，按類似年期以類
           with a similar security, the funds necessary to obtain           似抵押品借入必要資金所須支付的利
           an asset of a similar value to the right-of-use asset in         率。因 此，增 量 借 貸 利 率 反 映 本 集 團
           a similar economic environment. The IBR therefore                「應支付」的利率，當沒有可觀察的利率
           reflects what the Group”would have to pay”, which                時（例如沒有訂立融資交易的附屬公司）
           requires estimation when no observable rates are                 或需要調整以反映租賃條款及條件（例
           available (such as for subsidiaries that do not enter into       如租賃並非以附屬公司的功能貨幣訂
           financing transactions) or when it needs to be adjusted          立），便需要作出估計。本集團使用可
           to reflect the terms and conditions of the lease (for            觀察輸入值（例如市場利率）來估計增
           example, when leases are not in the subsidiary’s                 量借貸利率，並須作出若干實體特定的
           functional currency). The Group estimates the IBR                估計（例如附屬公司的獨立信貸評級）。
           using observable inputs (such as market interest rates)
           when available and is required to make certain
           entity-specific estimates (such as the subsidiary’s
           stand-alone credit rating).

   (b)   Performance obligations                                 (b)   履約義務
         Information about the Group’s performance                     本集團履約義務的資料概述如下：
         obligations is summarised below:

         General healthcare services                                   綜合醫療服務
         Revenue from the provision of general healthcare              提供綜合醫療服務產生的收益主
         services is mainly derived from hospital services             要來自於併表醫院提供醫院服
         provided at the consolidated hospitals, which is              務，其於向客戶提供相關服務時
         recognised when the related services have been                確認。
         rendered to customers.

         For in-patient services, customers normally                   對於住院服務，客戶通常接受住
         receive in-patient treatment which contains                   院治療，其中包含各種全部息息
         various treatment components that are all highly              相關及被視為單一履約義務的治
         interdependent and regarded as a single                       療部分。住院時間通常是約十一
         performance obligation. The period of in-patient              天之內。由於病人同時收取及消
         services is usually within about eleven days. Since           耗本集團進行醫務治療的利益，
         the patient simultaneously receives and consumes              住院服務的相關收益在合約期間
         the benefits of the Group’s performance in the                通過參考完成履約義務的進度而
         medical treatment, the relevant revenue of                    被確認。完成履約義務的進度乃
         in-patient services is recognised over the period             透過直接計量本集團向客戶轉讓
         of the contract by reference to the progress                  的個別產品或服務的價值來衡量。
         towards complete satisfaction of that
         performance obligation. The progress towards the
         complete satisfaction of performance obligation is
         measured by direct measurements of the value of
         individual products or services transferred by the
         Group to the customer.

         Revenue from the provision of out-patient                     提供門診服務的收益在某個時間
         services is recognised at a point in time, i.e.               點（即提供服務時）確認。
         when the services are provided.

         Hospital management services                                  醫院管理服務
         The Group provides comprehensive hospital                     本集團向IOT醫院、舉辦權醫院及
         management services to the IOT hospitals, the                 OT醫院提供綜合醫院管理服務並
         Sponsored Hospitals and OT hospitals and                      收取管理費。本集團亦通過建立

         hospitals. The Group also receives supply chain               應商收取供應鏈管理服務費。
         management service fees from third party
         suppliers through setting up a supply chain

      (Note)                                                   價值（附註）                       65,604           65,301

   Note:                                                                           附註：


   The listed equity investment was classified as financial assets at FVTPL as     由於本集團選擇不透過其他全面收入確認公允價值
   the Group has not elected to recognise the fair value gain or loss through      收益或虧損，故上市股本投資分類為以公允價值計
   other comprehensive income. It was held for long-term strategic purposes.       量且其變動計入損益的金融資產，乃持有作長期戰
   The Group does not intend to dispose of it within twelve months after the       略用途。本集團無意於報告期後十二個月內處置。
   reporting period.



   The fair value of the Group’s listed equity investment                          本集團於其財務報表批核日期之上市股

           Agreement by Yan Hua Phoenix and Yan Hua Hospital on 21 January
           2019 shall be void.


           Having considered the terms of the Yan Hua IOT Agreement, the Group            考慮到燕化IOT協議的條款，本集團進一步要求燕化
           has further claimed that Yan Hua Phoenix and Yan Hua Hospital should be        鳳凰及燕化醫院須支付違反燕化IOT協議之違約金。
           liable for damages for breach of the Yan Hua IOT Agreement. Accordingly,       據此，本集團將對燕化醫院IOT安排下的相關應收款
           the Group classified the related receivables and operating right under the     項和相關經營權分類為其他應收款項。
           IOT arrangement with Yan Hua Hospital as other receivable.


           On 18 December 2019, the Beijing Second Intermediate People’s Court            於2019年12月18日，北京市第二中級人民法院宣佈
           issued a judgement in favour of the Group. Yan Hua Phoenix and Yan Hua         有關起訴結果，本集團勝訴。燕化鳳凰和燕化醫院已
           Hospital filed an appeal to the Beijing Higher People’s Court in January       於2020年1月 向 北 京 市 高 級 人 民 法 院 提 起 上 訴。於
           2020. On 22 November 2021, the Beijing Higher People’s Court handed            2021年11月22日，北 京 市 高 級 人 民 法 院 頒 佈 判 決
           down a judgment (2020 Jing Minzhong No.110) which rejected the appeal         （2020京民終110號），駁回燕化鳳凰和燕化醫院提起
           filed by Yan Hua Phoenix and Yan Hua Hospital and upheld the original          的上訴，維持原判決，並裁定此判決為終審判決。
           judgment, and adjudged that such ruling shall be final.

48. FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                    48. 財務風險管理目標及政策
    OBJECTIVES AND POLICIES
  The Group’s principal financial instruments comprise             本集團的主要金融工具包括現金及現金
  cash and cash equivalents, restricted and pledged bank           等價物、限制性和已抵押銀行存款、其
  deposits, other financial assets at amortised cost,              他按攤銷成本計量的金融資產、以公允
  financial assets at FVTPL, payables to the Sponsored             價值計量且其變動計入損益的金融資
  Hospitals and interest-bearing bank and other                    產、應付舉辦權醫院款項以及計息銀行
  borrowings. The main purpose of these financial                  借款及其他借款。這些金融工具的主要
  instruments is to finance the Group’s operations. The            目的是為本集團的運營提供資金。本集
  Group has various other financial assets and liabilities         團還有其他各種金融資產和負債，例如
  such as receivables from IOT hospitals, loan to a                來自IOT醫院之應收款項、向一間舉辦
  sponsored hospital, trade and bills receivables, financial       權醫院提供的貸款、應收貿易款項及應
  assets included in prepayments, deposits and other               收票據、計入預付款項、保證金及其他
  receivables, balances with related parties, trade and            應 收 款 項 的 金 融 資 產、與 關 聯 方 的 結
  bills payables, financial liabilities included in other          餘、應付貿易款項及應付票據、計入其
  payables and accruals, and lease liabilities, which              他應付款項及應計費用的金融負債，以
  mainly arise directly from its operations.                       及 租 賃 負 債，主 要 直 接 來 自 其 經 營 活
                                                                   動。

  The main risks arising from the Group’s financial                本集團金融工具產生的主要風險是利率
  instruments are interest rate risk, foreign currency risk,       風險、外匯風險、信貸風險、流動資金
  credit risk, liquidity risk and equity price risk. The board     風險及權益價格風險。董事會審查並同
  of directors reviews and agrees policies for managing            意管理每種風險的政策，現概述如下。
  each of these risks and they are summarised below.

  Interest rate risk                                               利率風險
  The Group’s exposure to the risk of changes in market            本集團承受的市場利率變動風險主要與
  interest rates relates primarily to the Group’s                  本集團具有浮動利率的計息銀行借款有
  interest-bearing bank borrowings with floating interest          關。本集團並無使用衍生金融工具對沖
  rates. The Group does not use derivative financial               其利率風險。本集團通過密切監控利率
  instruments to hedge its interest rate risk. The Group           變動並定期審查其可用信貸額度及其使
  mitigates this risk by closely monitoring the                    用情況來減輕這種風險。
  movements in interest rates and reviewing its available
  credit facilities and their utilisation regularly.

      48. FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                           48. 財務風險管理目標及政策（續）
          OBJECTIVES AND POLICIES (continued)
           Liquidity risk                                                               流動資金風險
           Liquidity risk is the risk that the Group will encounter                     流動資金風險指本集團因資金短缺而難
           difficulty in meeting financial obligations due to                           以履行財務責任的風險。本集團面臨的
           shortage of funds. The Group’s exposure to liquidity                         流動資金風險主要來自金融資產和負債
           risk arises primarily from mismatches of the maturities                      的到期日錯配。本集團通過考慮金融負
           of financial assets and liabilities. The Group monitors                      債和金融資產的到期日來監控資金短缺
           its risk to a shortage of funds by considering the                           的風險。
           maturities of both its financial liabilities and financial
           assets.

           The Group’s objective is to maintain a balance                               本集團的目標是通過使用銀行借款及其

              which are not-for-profit hospitals, have respective obligations to                              的舉辦人各有義務向該等機構注入初始資本。
              contribute the start-up capital to such institutions. Such start-up                             初始資本一經注入，舉辦人即不可提取。基

              income of such hospitals shall only be used in line with its                                    用）根 據 有 關 醫 院 的 組 織 章 程 細 則 使 用，因
              purpose and within its business scope, and where applicable, in                                 此，收入淨額不可分派予舉辦人作為股息，
              accordance with the articles of association of such hospitals,                                  與擁有一間公司股權的股東不同。
              therefore, the net income is not distributable to its sponsors as
              dividends, which is different from a shareholder who owns the
              equity interests of a company.
